# #1073: OmniRoute June 11–20, 2026 row dispositions

Reviewed 2026-11-20. This is a source-metadata and bounded static comparison report for the inclusive UTC window 2026-06-11 through 2026-06-20. It is not an owner-selection list.

## Verified inventory and completeness

The public GitHub search query was `repo:cheaper-inference/OmniRoute is:pr is:merged merged:2026-06-11..2026-06-20`; its total was 590. Independent non-overlapping UTC slices returned 212 for 2026-06-11..2026-06-15 and 378 for 2026-06-16..2026-06-20. Their sum is 590. The paged result inventory contains 590 distinct PR numbers (no duplicate numbers). Each row below was checked against the pull-request detail endpoint: the exact `merged_at` is in the requested interval, and both source head SHA and merge commit SHA are recorded.

The final changed-file list was fetched with pagination for every row. For all 590 PRs, the number of returned file entries equals the PR detail `changed_files` count; no row has missing file pagination in the collected public metadata. The path column lists every changed path, not a sample. The API path list is metadata evidence only: it does not establish the behavior of a code change.

This supplements and reconciles [`1073-first-quarter-merged-source-scope.md`](1073-first-quarter-merged-source-scope.md), [`1073-may-merged-source-scope.md`](1073-may-merged-source-scope.md), and [`1073-june-merged-source-scope.md`](1073-june-merged-source-scope.md). The previous June note supplied the same 590 count but no row inventory; this report independently re-queries the requested window and records every row. Existing disposition reports and `internal/docs/upstream-sync.mdx` were consulted for prior local decisions. No source PR is called already-present merely because a path or title resembles local code. A confirmed existing disposition below has explicit diff-to-local evidence; all other code rows remain unresolved.

## Dispositions

“Unresolved” means the exact source identity, merge time, SHAs, and complete changed paths are known, but this review did not establish behavioral applicability or local parity. “Provider Sign-in” is reserved for rows whose changed paths identify provider onboarding/authentication or an added provider executor; mixed rows retain an explicit unresolved remainder. Docs-only and workflow-only exclusions are based on their complete path lists. No decision relies on a title or label to infer runtime behavior.

## Narrow final-diff checks of existing surfaces

These three rows were selected from existing-provider/protocol paths. Their upstream final diffs were read and compared to the current JavaScript implementation. A changed path, title, or related local module is not treated as proof of parity. The notes below record only observed differences and the focused evidence still needed; they do not authorize a feature design.

| Source PR and exact source head | Final source diff behavior | Current local comparison and disposition |
|---|---|---|
| [#3691](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3691), `a935cd54cb3a98eca2b0edfa209b5273eff711db` | The final diff changes `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-claude.ts` so `top_p` is forwarded only when `temperature` is absent, and strips `top_p` in the chat-core Claude destination path when both are present. Its focused test asserts both fields together. | Local `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-claude.js:64–65` forwards `temperature` but has no `top_p` mapping; `:260–262` handles temperature removal for thinking. Therefore the exact dual-field rejection is not present on this translator path, but local also does not preserve the source fix's top_p-only case. **Unresolved protocol compatibility delta**, not “already present.” Focused unit target: `tests/unit/translator-openai-to-claude.test.js` (not present in this checkout); add/identify the local focused test for top_p-only preservation and both-fields behavior before closing this row. No runtime request was sent.
| [#3704](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3704), `23360c413cbfbb813e60b5b9d6dca2b56ae534f9` | The final diff changes the registered OpenAI→Gemini route from native to context mode for signatureless history, while retaining the signed-call native path in focused tests. | Local `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.js:244–284` emits historical function-call parts and attaches a decoded, cached, or synthetic default signature for non-Gemma-4 models; no context-mode fallback is visible in this block. This is an observable source/local strategy difference, but this static comparison does not establish whether the local signature is accepted on the same destination/model cases. **Unresolved.** Focused unit target: `tests/unit/translator-openai-to-gemini.test.js`; assert the registered signatureless case and signed-history preservation using offline inputs. No provider was contacted.
| [#3689](https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/pull/3689), `1be4ab1b77c829c73f932b87bd4f6e85ff75b02e` | The final diff changes the Claude-source empty-stream flush path to emit a 502 error and abort instead of synthesizing a successful empty Claude response. | The local `open-sse/translator/response/claude-to-openai.js:19–30` emits a synthetic whitespace content delta for OpenAI-compatible clients when a terminal arrives with no content/tool calls; it is not the same source format or flush seam. This does not prove parity or a gap for the upstream Claude-source path. **Unresolved.** Focused unit target: the existing `tests/unit/claude-empty-thinking-block.test.js` covers the local empty/thinking stream policy; a focused zero-event Claude-source flush/error-terminal assertion is not established by that test name or the local comparison. No stream was probed.

## Limits

The 590 rows are complete as public merge metadata and changed-file inventories, but most rows have not had their final diffs compared with local behavior. “Unresolved” is intentionally not converted into an applicability finding. New provider/authentication paths are routed to Provider Sign-in; paths alone do not establish runtime behavior. Public documentation and workflow-only changes are excluded only where their full changed-file lists contain no other paths. Existing dashboard paths remain unresolved unless the changed row is demonstrably a fix to an existing page; a new page would not port under this scope. Dependency, security, and broad mixed release rows remain unresolved unless the evidence above supports a narrower disposition. No scratch code was executed, and no repository baseline, workflow, product source, test, credential, provider, or networked application behavior was changed or exercised.
